<div id="LC61" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">its Starshield military counterpart. In 2020, SpaceX Subsidiaries Starlink[9]</div>
<div id="LC62" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC63" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">began to operate its Dragon 2 capsules to deliver Swarm Technologies</div>
<div id="LC64" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">crewed missions for NASA and private entities. Pioneer Aerospace</div>
<div id="LC65" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Around this time, SpaceX began building test</div>
<div id="LC66" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC67" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Website spacex.com (https://www.spac</div>
<div id="LC68" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">prototypes for Starship, which is the largest launch ex.com/)</div>
<div id="LC69" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicle in history and aims to fully realize the</div>
<div id="LC70" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company&#39;s vision of a fully reusable, cost-effective</div>
<div id="LC71" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and adaptable launch vehicle. SpaceX is also developing its own space suit and astronaut via its Polaris</div>
<div id="LC72" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">program[13] as well as developing the human lander for lunar missions under NASA&#39;s Artemis</div>
<div id="LC73" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">program.[14] SpaceX is not publicly traded; a space industry newspaper estimated that SpaceX has a</div>
<div id="LC74" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">revenue of over $10 billion in 2024.[4]</div>
<div id="LC75" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC76" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">History</div>
<div id="LC77" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC78" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2001–2004: Founding</div>
<div id="LC79" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In early 2001, Elon Musk met Robert Zubrin and donated $100,000 to his Mars Society, joining its board</div>
<div id="LC80" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of directors for a short time.[15]: 30–31  He gave a plenary talk at their fourth convention where he</div>
<div id="LC81" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">announced Mars Oasis, a project to land a greenhouse and grow plants on Mars.[16][17] Musk initially</div>
<div id="LC82" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">attempted to acquire a Dnepr launch vehicle for the project through Russian contacts from Jim</div>
<div id="LC83" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Cantrell[18].</div>
<div id="LC84" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC85" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk returned with his team to Moscow, this time bringing Michael Griffin, who later became the 11th</div>
<div id="LC86" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Administrator of NASA, but found the Russians increasingly unreceptive.[19][20] On the flight home,</div>
<div id="LC87" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk announced he could start a company to build the affordable rockets they needed instead.[20] By</div>
<div id="LC88" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">applying vertical integration,[19] using inexpensive commercial off-the-shelf components when</div>
<div id="LC89" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">possible,[20] and adopting the modular approach of modern software engineering, Musk believed SpaceX</div>
<div id="LC90" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">could significantly cut launch costs.[20]</div>
<div id="LC91" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC92" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In early 2002, Elon Musk started to look for staff for his company, soon to be named SpaceX. Musk</div>
<div id="LC93" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">approached five people for the initial positions at the fledgling company, including Griffin, who declined</div>
<div id="LC94" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the position of Chief Engineer,[21]: 11  Jim Cantrell and John Garvey (Cantrell and Garvey later founded</div>
<div id="LC95" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the company Vector Launch), rocket engineer Tom Mueller, and Chris Thompson.[21][22] SpaceX was</div>
<div id="LC96" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">first headquartered in a warehouse in El Segundo, California. Early SpaceX employees, such as Tom</div>
<div id="LC97" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Mueller (CTO), Gwynne Shotwell (COO), and Chris Thompson (VP of Operations), came from</div>
<div id="LC98" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">neighboring TRW and Boeing corporations. By November 2005, the company had 160 employees.[23]</div>
<div id="LC99" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC100" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk personally interviewed and approved all of SpaceX&#39;s early employees.[21]: 22 </div>
<div id="LC101" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC102" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk has stated that one of his goals with SpaceX is to decrease the cost and improve the reliability of</div>
<div id="LC103" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">access to space, ultimately by a factor of ten.[24]</div>
<div id="LC104" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC105" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC106" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC107" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2005–2009: Falcon 1 and first orbital launches</div>
<div id="LC108" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX developed its first orbital launch vehicle, the Falcon</div>
<div id="LC109" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1, with internal funding.[25][26] The Falcon 1 was an</div>
<div id="LC110" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expendable two-stage-to-orbit small-lift launch vehicle. The</div>
<div id="LC111" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">total development cost of Falcon 1 was approximately</div>
<div id="LC112" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">$90 million[27] to $100 million.[21]: 215 </div>
<div id="LC113" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC114" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The Falcon rocket series was named after Star Wars &#39;s</div>
<div id="LC115" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Millennium Falcon fictional spacecraft.[28]</div>
<div id="LC116" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC117" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2004, SpaceX protested against NASA to the Government</div>
<div id="LC118" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Accountability Office (GAO) because of a sole-source The first successful Falcon 1 launch in</div>
<div id="LC119" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC120" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">September 2008</div>
<div id="LC121" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contract awarded to Kistler Aerospace. Before the GAO could</div>
<div id="LC122" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">respond, NASA withdrew the contract, and formed the COTS</div>
<div id="LC123" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">program.[21]: 109–110 [29] In 2005, SpaceX announced plans to pursue a human-rated commercial space</div>
<div id="LC124" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">program through the end of the decade, a program that would later become the Dragon spacecraft.[30] In</div>
<div id="LC125" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2006, the company was selected by NASA and awarded $396 million to provide crew and cargo resupply</div>
<div id="LC126" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">demonstration contracts to the International Space Station (ISS) under the COTS program.[31]</div>
<div id="LC127" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC128" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first two Falcon 1 launches were purchased by the United States Department of Defense under the</div>
<div id="LC129" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">DARPA Falcon Project which evaluated new U.S. launch vehicles suitable for use in hypersonic missile</div>
<div id="LC130" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">delivery for Prompt Global Strike.[26][32][33] The first three launches of the rocket, between 2006 and</div>
<div id="LC131" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2008, all resulted in failures, which almost ended the company. Financing for Tesla Motors had failed, as</div>
<div id="LC132" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">well,[34] and consequently Tesla, SolarCity, and Musk personally were all nearly bankrupt at the same</div>
<div id="LC133" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">time.[21]: 178–182  Musk was reportedly &quot;waking from nightmares, screaming and in physical pain&quot; because</div>
<div id="LC134" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the stress.[21]: 216 </div>
<div id="LC135" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC136" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The financial situation started to turn around with the first successful launch achieved on the fourth</div>
<div id="LC137" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">attempt on September 28, 2008. Musk split his remaining $30 million between SpaceX and Tesla, and</div>
<div id="LC138" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NASA awarded the first Commercial Resupply Services (CRS) contract awarding $1.6 billion to SpaceX</div>
<div id="LC139" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in December, thus financially saving the company.[21]: 217–221  Based on these factors and the further</div>
<div id="LC140" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">business operations they enabled, the Falcon 1 was soon retired following its second successful, and fifth</div>
<div id="LC141" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">total, launch in July 2009. This allowed SpaceX to focus company resources on the development of a</div>
<div id="LC142" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">larger orbital rocket, the Falcon 9.[35] Gwynne Shotwell was also promoted to company president at the</div>
<div id="LC143" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">time, for her role in successfully negotiating the CRS contract with the NASA Associate Administrator</div>
<div id="LC144" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Bill Gerstenmaier.[36][21]: 222 </div>
<div id="LC145" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC146" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2010–2012: Falcon 9, Dragon, and NASA contracts</div>
<div id="LC147" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX originally intended to follow its light Falcon 1 launch vehicle with an intermediate capacity</div>
<div id="LC148" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicle, the Falcon 5.[37] The company instead decided in 2005 to proceed with the development of the</div>
<div id="LC149" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 9, a reusable heavier lift vehicle. Development of the Falcon 9 was accelerated by NASA, which</div>
<div id="LC150" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">committed to purchasing several commercial flights if specific capabilities were demonstrated. This</div>
<div id="LC151" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">started with seed money from the Commercial Orbital Transportation Services (COTS) program in</div>
<div id="LC152" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2006.[38] The overall contract award was $278 million to provide development funding for the Dragon</div>
<div id="LC153" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC154" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC155" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC156" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft, Falcon 9, and demonstration launches of Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC157" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with Dragon.[38] As part of this contract, the Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC158" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launched for the first time in June 2010 with the Dragon</div>
<div id="LC159" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Spacecraft Qualification Unit, using a mockup of the Dragon</div>
<div id="LC160" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft.</div>
<div id="LC161" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC162" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first operational Dragon spacecraft was launched in</div>
<div id="LC163" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">December 2010 aboard COTS Demo Flight 1, the Falcon 9&#39;s 0:00</div>
<div id="LC164" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC165" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">second flight, and safely returned to Earth after two orbits,</div>
<div id="LC166" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">completing all its mission objectives.[39] By December 2010,</div>
<div id="LC167" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the SpaceX production line was manufacturing one Falcon 9 Video of the first launch of Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC168" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC169" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and Dragon every three months.[40]</div>
<div id="LC170" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC171" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2011, as part of its second-round Commercial Crew Development (CCDev) program, NASA</div>
<div id="LC172" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">issued a $75 million contract for SpaceX to develop an integrated launch escape system for Dragon in</div>
<div id="LC173" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">preparation for human-rating it as a crew transport vehicle to the ISS.[41] NASA awarded SpaceX a fixed-</div>
<div id="LC174" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">price Space Act Agreement (SAA) to produce a detailed design of the crew transportation system in</div>
<div id="LC175" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">August 2012.[42]</div>
<div id="LC176" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC177" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In early 2012, approximately two-thirds of SpaceX stock was owned by Musk[43] and his seventy million</div>
<div id="LC178" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">shares were then estimated to be worth $875  million on private markets,[44] valuing SpaceX at</div>
<div id="LC179" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">$1.3  billion.[45] In May 2012, with the Dragon C2+ launch, Dragon became the first commercial</div>
<div id="LC180" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft to deliver cargo to the International Space Station.[46] After the flight, the company private</div>
<div id="LC181" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">equity valuation nearly doubled to $2.4 billion or $20/share.[47][48] By that time, SpaceX had operated on</div>
<div id="LC182" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">total funding of approximately $1  billion over its first decade of operation. Of this, private equity</div>
<div id="LC183" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">provided approximately $200  million, with Musk investing approximately $100  million and other</div>
<div id="LC184" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">investors having put in about $100 million.[49]</div>
<div id="LC185" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC186" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s active reusability test program began in late 2012 with testing low-altitude, low-speed aspects</div>
<div id="LC187" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the landing technology.[50] The Falcon 9 prototypes performed vertical takeoffs and landings (VTOL).</div>
<div id="LC188" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">High-velocity, high-altitude tests of the booster atmospheric return technology began in late 2013.[50]</div>
<div id="LC189" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC190" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2013–2015: Commercial launches and rapid growth</div>
<div id="LC191" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX launched the first commercial mission for a private customer in 2013. In 2014, SpaceX won nine</div>
<div id="LC192" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contracts out of the 20 that were openly competed worldwide.[51] That year Arianespace requested that</div>
<div id="LC193" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">European governments provide additional subsidies to face the competition from SpaceX.[52][53]</div>
<div id="LC194" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC195" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Beginning in 2014, SpaceX capabilities and pricing also began to affect the market for launch of U.S.</div>
<div id="LC196" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">military payloads, which for nearly a decade had been dominated by the large U.S. launch provider</div>
<div id="LC197" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">United Launch Alliance (ULA).[54] The monopoly had allowed launch costs by the U.S. provider to rise</div>
<div id="LC198" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to over $400 million over the years.[55] In September 2014, NASA&#39;s Director of Commercial Spaceflight,</div>
<div id="LC199" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Kevin Crigler, awarded SpaceX the Commercial Crew Transportation Capability (CCtCap) contract to</div>
<div id="LC200" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">finalize the development of the Crew Transportation System. The contract included several technical and</div>
<div id="LC201" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">certification milestones, an uncrewed flight test, a crewed flight test, and six operational missions after</div>
<div id="LC202" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">certification.[42]</div>
<div id="LC203" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC204" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC205" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC206" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2015, SpaceX raised $1 billion in funding from Google and</div>
<div id="LC207" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Fidelity Investments, in exchange for 8.33% of the company,</div>
<div id="LC208" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">establishing the company valuation at approximately $12  billion.[56]</div>
<div id="LC209" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC210" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The same month SpaceX announced the development of a new satellite</div>
<div id="LC211" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">constellation, called Starlink, to provide global broadband internet</div>
<div id="LC212" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">service with 4,000 satellites.[57]</div>
<div id="LC213" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC214" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The Falcon 9 had its first major failure in late June 2015, when the</div>
<div id="LC215" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">seventh ISS resupply mission, CRS-7 exploded two minutes into the</div>
<div id="LC216" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">flight. The problem was traced to a failed two-foot-long steel strut that</div>
<div id="LC217" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">held a helium pressure vessel, which broke free due to the force of</div>
<div id="LC218" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">acceleration. This caused a breach and allowed high-pressure helium to</div>
<div id="LC219" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">escape into the low-pressure propellant tank, causing the failure.[58]</div>
<div id="LC220" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC221" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2015–2017: Reusability milestones Launch of Falcon 9 carrying</div>
<div id="LC222" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ORBCOMM OG2-M1, July</div>
<div id="LC223" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC224" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX first achieved a successful landing and recovery of a first stage 2014</div>
<div id="LC225" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in December 2015 with Falcon 9 Flight 20.[59] In April 2016, the</div>
<div id="LC226" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company achieved the first successful landing on the</div>
<div id="LC227" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">autonomous spaceport drone ship (ASDS) Of Course I Still</div>
<div id="LC228" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Love You in the Atlantic Ocean.[60] By October 2016,</div>
<div id="LC229" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">following the successful landings, SpaceX indicated they</div>
<div id="LC230" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">were offering their customers a 10% price discount if they</div>
<div id="LC231" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">choose to fly their payload on a reused Falcon 9 first</div>
<div id="LC232" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">stage.[61]</div>
<div id="LC233" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC234" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A second major rocket failure happened in early September</div>
<div id="LC235" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2016, when a Falcon 9 exploded during a propellant fill Falcon 9 first stage on an autonomous</div>
<div id="LC236" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operation for a standard pre-launch static fire test. The spaceport drone ship (ASDS) barge after</div>
<div id="LC237" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC238" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">payload, the AMOS-6 communications satellite valued at the first successful landing at sea,</div>
<div id="LC239" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC240" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">$200 million, was destroyed.[62] The explosion was caused by SpaceX CRS-8 mission</div>
<div id="LC241" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC242" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the liquid oxygen that is used as propellant turning so cold</div>
<div id="LC243" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">that it solidified and ignited with carbon composite helium vessels.[63] Though not considered an</div>
<div id="LC244" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">unsuccessful flight, the rocket explosion sent the company into a four-month launch hiatus while it</div>
<div id="LC245" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">worked out what went wrong. SpaceX returned to flight in January 2017.[64]</div>
<div id="LC246" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC247" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In March 2017, SpaceX launched a returned Falcon 9 for the SES-10 satellite. This was the first time a</div>
<div id="LC248" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">re-launch of a payload-carrying orbital rocket went back to space.[65] The first stage was recovered again,</div>
<div id="LC249" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">also making it the first landing of a reused orbital class rocket.[66]</div>
<div id="LC250" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC251" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2017–2018: Leading global commercial launch provider</div>
<div id="LC252" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2017, the company raised $350  million, which raised its valuation to $21  billion.[67] In 2017,</div>
<div id="LC253" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX achieved a 45% global market share for awarded commercial launch contracts.[68] By March</div>
<div id="LC254" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2018, SpaceX had more than 100 launches on its manifest representing about $12  billion in contract</div>
<div id="LC255" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">revenue.[69] The contracts included both commercial and government (NASA/DOD) customers.[70] This</div>
<div id="LC256" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">made SpaceX the leading global commercial launch provider measured by manifested launches.[71]</div>
<div id="LC257" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC258" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC259" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC260" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2017, SpaceX formed a subsidiary, The Boring Company,[72] and began work to construct a short test</div>
<div id="LC261" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">tunnel on and adjacent to the SpaceX headquarters and manufacturing facility, using a small number of</div>
<div id="LC262" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX employees,[73] which was completed in May 2018,[74] and opened to the public in December</div>
<div id="LC263" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2018.[75] During 2018, The Boring Company was spun out into a separate corporate entity with 6% of the</div>
<div id="LC264" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">equity going to SpaceX, less than 10% to early employees, and the remainder of the equity to Elon</div>
<div id="LC265" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk.[75]</div>
<div id="LC266" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC267" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Since 2019: Starship, first crewed launches, Starlink and general</div>
<div id="LC268" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2019 SpaceX raised $1.33  billion of capital across three funding rounds.[76] By May 2019, the</div>
<div id="LC269" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">valuation of SpaceX had risen to $33.3 billion[77] and reached $36 billion by March 2020.[78]</div>
<div id="LC270" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC271" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On August 19, 2020, after a $1.9 billion funding round, one of the largest single fundraising pushes by</div>
<div id="LC272" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">any privately held company, SpaceX&#39;s valuation increased to $46 billion.[79][80][81]</div>
<div id="LC273" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC274" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In February 2021, SpaceX raised an additional $1.61 billion in an equity round from 99 investors[82] at a</div>
<div id="LC275" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">per share value of approximately $420,[81] raising the company valuation to approximately $74 billion.</div>
<div id="LC276" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">By 2021, SpaceX had raised more than $6 billion in equity financing. Most of the capital raised since</div>
<div id="LC277" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2019 has been used to support the operational fielding of the Starlink satellite constellation and the</div>
<div id="LC278" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development and manufacture of the Starship launch vehicle.[82] By October 2021, the valuation of</div>
<div id="LC279" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX had risen to $100.3 billion.[83] On April 16, 2021, Starship HLS won a contract to play a critical</div>
<div id="LC280" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">role in the NASA crewed spaceflight Artemis program.[84] By 2021, SpaceX had entered into agreements</div>
<div id="LC281" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with Google Cloud Platform and Microsoft Azure to provide on-ground computer and networking</div>
<div id="LC282" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">services for Starlink.[85] A new round of financing in 2022 valued SpaceX at $127 billion.[86]</div>
<div id="LC283" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC284" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2021, SpaceX unveiled another drone ship named A Shortfall of Gravitas, landing a booster from</div>
<div id="LC285" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">CRS-23 on it for the first time on August 29, 2021.[87] Within the first 130 days of 2022, SpaceX had 18</div>
<div id="LC286" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rocket launches and two astronaut splashdowns. On December 13, 2021, company CEO Elon Musk</div>
<div id="LC287" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">announced that the company was starting a carbon dioxide removal program that would convert captured</div>
<div id="LC288" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">carbon into rocket fuel,[88][89] after he announced a $100 million donation to the X Prize Foundation the</div>
<div id="LC289" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">previous February to provide the monetary rewards to winners in a contest to develop the best carbon</div>
<div id="LC290" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">capture technology.[90][91]</div>
<div id="LC291" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC292" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In August 2022, Reuters reported that the European Space Agency (ESA) began initial discussions with</div>
<div id="LC293" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX that could lead to the company&#39;s launchers being used temporarily, given that Russia blocked</div>
<div id="LC294" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">access to Soyuz rockets amid the Russian invasion of Ukraine.[92] Since that invasion and in the greater</div>
<div id="LC295" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">war between Russia and Ukraine, Starlink was extensively used.[93]</div>
<div id="LC296" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC297" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2022, SpaceX&#39;s Falcon 9 also became the world record holder for the most launches of a single vehicle</div>
<div id="LC298" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">type in a single year.[94][95] SpaceX launched a rocket approximately every six days in 2022, with 61</div>
<div id="LC299" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launches in total. All but one (a Falcon Heavy in November) was on a Falcon 9 rocket.[94]</div>
<div id="LC300" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC301" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In November 2023, SpaceX announced it would acquire its parachute supplier Pioneer Aerospace out of</div>
<div id="LC302" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">bankruptcy for $2.2 million.[96][97]</div>
<div id="LC303" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC304" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 16, 2024, Elon Musk posted on X that SpaceX would move its headquarters from Hawthorne,</div>
<div id="LC305" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">California, to SpaceX Starbase in Brownsville, Texas. Musk said this was because the recently passed</div>
<div id="LC306" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">California AB1955 bill &quot;and the many others that preceded it, attacking both families and companies&quot;.[98]</div>
<div id="LC307" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC308" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC309" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC310" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This new law in California bans school districts from requiring that teachers notify parents about changes</div>
<div id="LC311" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to a student&#39;s sexual orientation and gender identity.[99] The headquarters officially moved to</div>
<div id="LC312" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Brownsville, Texas in August 2024, according to records filed with the California Secretary of State.[100]</div>
<div id="LC313" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC314" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The move to relocate SpaceX&#39;s headquarters was seen as largely symbolic, at least in the short term. The</div>
<div id="LC315" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hawthorne facility continues to support the company&#39;s Falcon launch vehicles, which was SpaceX&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC316" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">workhorse product in 2024.[101]</div>
<div id="LC317" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC318" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s 2024 Polaris Dawn mission featured the first-ever private spacewalk, marking a major</div>
<div id="LC319" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">milestone in commercial space exploration.[102]</div>
<div id="LC320" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC321" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2025 ProPublica reported that Chinese investors were investing in SpaceX via offshore entities, such</div>
<div id="LC322" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">as the Cayman Islands. Experts speculated that this might raise national security concerns with</div>
<div id="LC323" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">regulators.[103]</div>
<div id="LC324" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC325" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">By July 2025, as part of $5 Billion equity raise SpaceX agreed to invest $2 billion in xAI.[104]</div>
<div id="LC326" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC327" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starship</div>
<div id="LC328" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2019, SpaceX announced it would lay off 10% of its workforce to help finance the Starship</div>
<div id="LC329" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and Starlink projects.[105] The purpose of the Starship vehicle is to enable large-scale transit of humans</div>
<div id="LC330" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and cargo to the Moon, Mars, and beyond.[106] SpaceX&#39;s Starship is the largest and most powerful rocket</div>
<div id="LC331" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ever flown, with a planned payload capacity of 100+ tons.[107][108] Construction of initial prototypes and</div>
<div id="LC332" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">tests for Starship started in early 2019 in Florida and Texas. All Starship construction and testing moved</div>
<div id="LC333" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to the new SpaceX South Texas launch site later that year.</div>
<div id="LC334" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC335" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On April 20, 2023, Starship&#39;s first orbital flight test ended in a mid-air explosion over the Gulf of Mexico</div>
<div id="LC336" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">before booster separation. After launch, multiple engines in the booster progressively failed, causing the</div>
<div id="LC337" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicle to reach max q later than planned. &quot;Max q&quot; is the theoretical point of maximal mechanical stress</div>
<div id="LC338" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">which occurs during the launch sequence of a space vehicle. In the case of a rocket that must be self-</div>
<div id="LC339" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">destructed during its ascent, max q occurs at the point of self-destruction. Eventually, the vehicle lost</div>
<div id="LC340" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">control and spun erratically until the automated flight termination system was activated, which</div>
<div id="LC341" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">intentionally destroyed the rocket. Elon Musk, SpaceX, and other individuals familiar with the space</div>
<div id="LC342" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">industry have referred to the test flight as a success.[109][110]</div>
<div id="LC343" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC344" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Musk said at the time that it would take &quot;six to eight weeks&quot; to get the infrastructure prepared for another</div>
<div id="LC345" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launch. In October 2023, a senior SpaceX executive stated the company had been ready to launch the</div>
<div id="LC346" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">next test flight since September. He accused government regulators of disrupting the project&#39;s progress,</div>
<div id="LC347" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">adding the delay could lead to China beating U.S. astronauts back to the Moon.[111][112]</div>
<div id="LC348" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC349" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On November 18, 2023, SpaceX launched Starship on its second flight test, with both vehicles flying for</div>
<div id="LC350" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a few minutes before separately exploding.[113][114][115][116]</div>
<div id="LC351" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC352" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In early March 2024 SpaceX announced that it was targeting March 14 as the tentative launch date for its</div>
<div id="LC353" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">next uncrewed Starship launch configuration flight test, pending the issuance of a &quot;launch license&quot; by the</div>
<div id="LC354" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">FAA. This license was granted on March 13, 2024.[117] On March 14, 2024, at 13:25 UTC, Starship</div>
<div id="LC355" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC356" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC357" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC358" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launched for the third time and for the first time Starship reached its</div>
<div id="LC359" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">planned suborbital trajectory. The flight ended with the booster</div>
<div id="LC360" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">experiencing a malfunction shortly before landing and the ship being</div>
<div id="LC361" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">lost during re-entry over the Indian Ocean.[107][108]</div>
<div id="LC362" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC363" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On June 4, 2024, SpaceX received the launch license for Starship&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC364" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">fourth flight test. The licensure itself was notable in that it was the first</div>
<div id="LC365" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">time that the FAA included a clause that would allow SpaceX to launch</div>
<div id="LC366" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">subsequent test flights without a mishap investigation, provided that</div>
<div id="LC367" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">they met a similar launch profile and used the same specification of</div>
<div id="LC368" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">hardware. The provision could prove to speed the development</div>
<div id="LC369" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">timeline.[118]</div>
<div id="LC370" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC371" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On October 12, 2024, SpaceX received FAA approval for Starship&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC372" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">fifth flight test.[119] The flight was the first without engine failures, and</div>
<div id="LC373" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the first successful tower catch.[120]</div>
<div id="LC374" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC375" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX launched Starship on its sixth flight test on November 19,</div>
<div id="LC376" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2024.[121] The booster aborted the catch attempt, while the ship</div>
<div id="LC377" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">conducted a relight in space.[122][123]</div>
<div id="LC378" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC379" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On January 16, 2025, SpaceX launched Starship on its seventh flight</div>
<div id="LC380" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">test, with the first Block 2 Ship, Ship 33 (standing at 403  ft or 123</div>
<div id="LC381" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">meters). This test also carried a demonstration payload, a Starlink V3</div>
<div id="LC382" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">simulator. The test launched at 22:37 UTC. The test resulted in the</div>
<div id="LC383" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">second catch of the Super Heavy booster, B14, but after 8 minutes,</div>
<div id="LC384" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX lost contact with &#39;Ship&#39;, which is the upper stage of the Starship</div>
<div id="LC385" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">which resulted in the failure of the ship during the ascent. The</div>
<div id="LC386" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft reportedly exploded around 8.5 minutes after launch over</div>
<div id="LC387" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Atlantic Ocean near the Turks and Caicos Islands. The FAA, on Starship in launch position</div>
<div id="LC388" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">January 18, required a mishap investigation of the failure.[124]</div>
<div id="LC389" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC390" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On March 7, 2025, SpaceX launched another Starship rocket,</div>
<div id="LC391" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">this time from Texas. Contact was lost minutes into the test</div>
<div id="LC392" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">flight and the spacecraft came tumbling down and broke</div>
<div id="LC393" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">apart, with wreckage seen across Florida&#39;s skies.[125] As per</div>
<div id="LC394" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">preliminary investigation, Starship’s 7th test flight was</div>
<div id="LC395" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">disrupted by an oxygen leak, flashes and sustained fires in its</div>
<div id="LC396" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">aft section, which caused the rocket’s engines to shut down</div>
<div id="LC397" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and turn on the spacecraft’s self-destruct system.[126]</div>
<div id="LC398" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC399" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On June 18, 2025, a SpaceX Starship rocket exploded during Dragon&#39;s Flight: SpaceX Crew-10</div>
<div id="LC400" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launches aboard a SpaceX Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC401" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC402" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a static fire test at the company’s Starbase facility in Texas, rocket carrying the Dragon spacecraft</div>
<div id="LC403" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">following what the company described as a “major piloted by astronaut and Air Force Maj.</div>
<div id="LC404" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">anomaly”.[127]</div>

<div id="LC406" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Nichole &quot;Vapor&quot; Ayer at Kennedy Space</div>

<div id="LC411" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Crewed launches</div>
<div id="LC412" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A significant milestone was achieved in May 2020, when</div>
<div id="LC413" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX successfully launched two NASA astronauts (Doug</div>
<div id="LC414" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hurley and Bob Behnken) into orbit on a Crew Dragon</div>
<div id="LC415" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft during Crew Dragon Demo-2, making SpaceX the</div>

<div id="LC417" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Space Station and marking the first crewed orbital launch</div>

<div id="LC420" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in Florida.[130]</div>

<div id="LC429" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink satellites, beginning to deploy what would become</div>
<div id="LC430" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the world&#39;s largest commercial satellite constellation the following year.[131] In 2022, most SpaceX</div>
<div id="LC431" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launches focused on Starlink, a consumer internet business that sends batches of internet-beaming</div>
<div id="LC432" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">satellites and now has over 6,000 satellites in orbit.[132]</div>

<div id="LC439" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">7,500 of SpaceX&#39;s next-generation satellites in its Starlink internet network.[135]</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>
<div id="LC538" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">September 12, 2024 First commercial spacewalk Polaris Dawn</div>

</div>

</div>
<div id="LC542" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">First in space relight of a full-flow staged combustion cycle</div>
<div id="LC543" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">November 19, 2024</div>
<div id="LC544" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC545" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engine (Raptor).[122] Starship flight test 6</div>
<div id="LC546" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC547" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a. Excluding the passive objects launched as part of Project West Ford</div>
<div id="LC548" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC549" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hardware</div>
<div id="LC550" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC551" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Launch vehicles</div>
<div id="LC552" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX has developed three launch vehicles. The</div>
<div id="LC553" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">small-lift Falcon 1 was the first launch vehicle</div>
<div id="LC554" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">developed and was retired in 2009. The medium-lift</div>
<div id="LC555" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 9 and the heavy-lift Falcon Heavy are both</div>
<div id="LC556" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operational.</div>
<div id="LC557" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC558" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 1 was a small rocket capable of placing</div>
<div id="LC559" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">several hundred kilograms into low Earth orbit. It</div>
<div id="LC560" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launched five times between 2006 and 2009, of which</div>
<div id="LC561" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">two were successful.[156] The Falcon 1 was the first</div>
<div id="LC562" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">privately funded, liquid-fueled rocket to reach The landing of a Falcon 9 Block 5 first stage at</div>
<div id="LC563" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">orbit.[136] Cape Canaveral in July 2019. VTVL technologies</div>
<div id="LC564" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC565" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">are used in many of SpaceX&#39;s launch vehicles.</div>
<div id="LC566" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 9 is a medium-lift launch vehicle capable of</div>
<div id="LC567" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">delivering up to 22,800 kilograms (50,265 lb) to orbit,</div>
<div id="LC568" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">competing with the Delta IV and the Atlas V rockets, as well as other launch providers around the world.</div>
<div id="LC569" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">It has nine Merlin engines in its first stage. The Falcon 9 v1.0 rocket successfully reached orbit on its first</div>
<div id="LC570" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">attempt on June 4, 2010. Its third flight, COTS Demo Flight 2, launched on May 22, 2012, and launched</div>
<div id="LC571" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the first commercial spacecraft to reach and dock with the International Space Station (ISS).[46] The</div>
<div id="LC572" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicle was upgraded to Falcon 9 v1.1 in 2013, Falcon 9 Full Thrust in 2015, and finally to Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC573" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Block 5 in 2018. The first stage of Falcon 9 is designed to retro propulsively land, be recovered, and</div>
<div id="LC574" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">flown again.[157]</div>
<div id="LC575" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC576" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon Heavy is a heavy-lift launch vehicle capable of delivering up to 63,800 kg (140,700 lb) to Low</div>
<div id="LC577" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Earth orbit (LEO) or 26,700 kg (58,900 lb) to geosynchronous transfer orbit (GTO). It uses three slightly</div>
<div id="LC578" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">modified Falcon 9 first-stage cores with a total of 27 Merlin 1D engines.[158][159] The Falcon Heavy</div>
<div id="LC579" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">successfully flew its inaugural mission on February 6, 2018, launching Musk&#39;s personal Tesla Roadster</div>
<div id="LC580" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">into heliocentric orbit.[160]</div>
<div id="LC581" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC582" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC583" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC584" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Both the Falcon 9 and Falcon Heavy are certified to conduct launches for the National Security Space</div>
<div id="LC585" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Launch (NSSL).[161][162] As of August 22, 2025, the Falcon 9 and Falcon Heavy have been launched 529</div>
<div id="LC586" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">times, resulting in 526 full mission successes, one partial success, and one in-flight failure. In addition, a</div>
<div id="LC587" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 9 experienced a pre-flight failure before a static fire test in 2016.[163][164]</div>
<div id="LC588" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC589" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX is developing a fully reusable super-heavy lift launch system known as Starship. It comprises a</div>
<div id="LC590" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reusable first stage, called Super Heavy, and the reusable Starship second stage space vehicle. As of 2017,</div>
<div id="LC591" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the system was intended to supersede the company&#39;s existing launch vehicle hardware by the early</div>
<div id="LC592" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2020s.[165][166]</div>
<div id="LC593" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC594" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Rocket engines</div>
<div id="LC595" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Since the founding of SpaceX in 2002, the company</div>
<div id="LC596" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">has developed several rocket engines  – Merlin,</div>
<div id="LC597" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Kestrel, and Raptor  – for use in launch</div>
<div id="LC598" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicles,[167][168] Draco for the reaction control</div>
<div id="LC599" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">system of the Dragon series of spacecraft,[169] and</div>
<div id="LC600" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SuperDraco for abort capability in Crew Dragon.[170]</div>
<div id="LC601" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC602" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Merlin is a family of rocket engines that uses liquid</div>
<div id="LC603" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">oxygen (LOX) and RP-1 propellants. Merlin was first</div>
<div id="LC604" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">used to power the Falcon 1&#39;s first stage and is now</div>
<div id="LC605" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">used on both stages of the Falcon 9 and Falcon Heavy Merlin 1D engine undergoes a test at SpaceX&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC606" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">vehicles.[171] Kestrel uses the same propellants and Rocket Development and Test Facility in</div>
<div id="LC607" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC608" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was used as the Falcon 1 rocket&#39;s second-stage main McGregor, Texas</div>
<div id="LC609" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC610" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engine.[168][172]</div>
<div id="LC611" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC612" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Draco and SuperDraco are hypergolic liquid-propellant rocket engines. Draco engines are used on the</div>
<div id="LC613" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reaction control system of the Dragon and Dragon 2 spacecraft.[169] The SuperDraco engine is more</div>
<div id="LC614" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">powerful, and eight SuperDraco engines provide launch escape capability for crewed Dragon 2 spacecraft</div>
<div id="LC615" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">during an abort scenario.[173]</div>
<div id="LC616" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC617" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Raptor is a new family of liquid oxygen and liquid methane-fueled full-flow staged combustion cycle</div>
<div id="LC618" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engines to power the first and second stages of the in-development Starship launch system.[167]</div>
<div id="LC619" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC620" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Development versions were test-fired in late 2016,[174] and the engine flew for the first time in 2019,</div>
<div id="LC621" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">powering the Starhopper vehicle to an altitude of 20 m (66 ft).[175]</div>
<div id="LC622" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC623" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Dragon spacecraft</div>
<div id="LC624" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX has developed the Dragon spacecraft to transport cargo and crew to the International Space</div>
<div id="LC625" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Station (ISS).</div>
<div id="LC626" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC627" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first-generation Dragon 1 spacecraft was used only for cargo operations. It was developed with</div>
<div id="LC628" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">financial support from NASA under the Commercial Orbital Transportation Services (COTS) program.</div>
<div id="LC629" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">After a successful COTS demonstration flight in 2010, SpaceX was chosen to receive a Commercial</div>
<div id="LC630" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Resupply Services (CRS) contract.[39]</div>
<div id="LC631" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC632" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC633" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC634" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The currently operational second-generation Dragon 2</div>
<div id="LC635" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft conducted its first flight, without crew, to the ISS</div>
<div id="LC636" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in early 2019, followed by a crewed flight of Dragon 2 in</div>
<div id="LC637" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2020.[128] It was developed with financial support from</div>
<div id="LC638" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NASA under the Commercial Crew Program program. The</div>
