<div id="LC639" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cargo variant of Dragon 2 flew for the first time in December</div>
<div id="LC640" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2020, for a resupply to the ISS as part of the CRS contract</div>
<div id="LC641" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with NASA.[176]</div>
<div id="LC642" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC643" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In March 2020 SpaceX revealed the Dragon XL, designed as SpaceX Dragon 2 spacecraft, designed</div>
<div id="LC644" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC645" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a resupply spacecraft for NASA&#39;s planned Lunar Gateway to deliver crew and cargo to and from the</div>
<div id="LC646" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">International Space Station (cargo</div>
<div id="LC647" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC648" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">space station under a Gateway Logistics Services (GLS) version shown)</div>
<div id="LC649" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contract.[177] Dragon XL is planned to launch on the Falcon</div>
<div id="LC650" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Heavy, and is able to transport over 5,000 kg (11,000  lb) to</div>
<div id="LC651" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Gateway. Dragon XL will be docked at the Gateway for six to twelve months at a time.[178]</div>
<div id="LC652" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC653" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX designed a spacesuit to be worn inside the Dragon spacecraft to protect from possible</div>
<div id="LC654" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">depressurization.[179] On May 4, 2024, SpaceX unveiled a second spacesuit designed for extravehicular</div>
<div id="LC655" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">activity, planned to be used for a spacewalk during the Polaris Dawn mission.[180]</div>
<div id="LC656" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC657" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Autonomous spaceport drone ships</div>
<div id="LC658" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX routinely returns the first stage of Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC659" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and Falcon Heavy rockets after orbital launches. The</div>
<div id="LC660" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rocket lands at a predetermined landing site using</div>
<div id="LC661" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">only its propulsion systems.[181] When propellant</div>
<div id="LC662" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">margins do not permit a return to a launch site</div>
<div id="LC663" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(RTLS), rockets return to a floating landing platform</div>
<div id="LC664" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in the ocean, called autonomous spaceport drone ships</div>
<div id="LC665" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(ASDS).[182]</div>
<div id="LC666" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC667" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX also had plans to introduce floating launch</div>
<div id="LC668" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">platforms, which would be modified oil rigs provide a</div>
<div id="LC669" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sea launch option for their Starship launch vehicle. As Autonomous spaceport drone ship in position</div>
<div id="LC670" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC671" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of February 2023, SpaceX had sold the oil rigs, but prior to CRS-6 mission</div>
<div id="LC672" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC673" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">had not ruled out sea-based platforms for future</div>
<div id="LC674" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">use.[183]</div>
<div id="LC675" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC676" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink</div>
<div id="LC677" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink is an internet satellite constellation under development by Starlink Services, LLC, a wholly</div>
<div id="LC678" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">owned subsidiary of SpaceX,[9][184] that consists of thousands of cross-linked communications satellites</div>
<div id="LC679" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in ~550 km orbits. Its goal is to address the significant unmet demand worldwide for low-cost broadband</div>
<div id="LC680" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">capabilities.[185] Development began in 2015, and initial prototype test-flight satellites were launched on</div>
<div id="LC681" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the SpaceX Paz satellite mission in 2017. In May 2019, SpaceX launched the first batch of 60 satellites</div>
<div id="LC682" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">aboard a Falcon 9.[186] Initial test operation of the constellation began in late 2020[187] and first orders</div>
<div id="LC683" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC684" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC685" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC686" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">were taken in early 2021.[188] Customers were told to</div>
<div id="LC687" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expect internet service speeds of 50  Mbit/s to</div>
<div id="LC688" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">150 Mbit/s and latency from 20 ms to 40 ms.[189] In</div>
<div id="LC689" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">December 2022, Starlink reached over 1 million</div>
<div id="LC690" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">subscribers worldwide.[190]</div>
<div id="LC691" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC692" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The planned large number of Starlink satellites has</div>
<div id="LC693" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">been criticized by astronomers due to concerns over</div>
<div id="LC694" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">light pollution,[191][192][193] with the brightness of</div>
<div id="LC695" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink satellites in both optical and radio Sixty Starlink satellites stacked together before</div>
<div id="LC696" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC697" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">wavelengths interfering with scientific deployment</div>
<div id="LC698" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC699" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">observations.[194] In response, SpaceX has</div>
<div id="LC700" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">implemented several upgrades to Starlink satellites aimed at reducing their brightness.[195] The large</div>
<div id="LC701" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">number of satellites employed by Starlink also creates long-term dangers of space debris</div>
<div id="LC702" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">collisions.[196][197] However, the satellites are equipped with krypton-fueled Hall thrusters which allow</div>
<div id="LC703" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">them to de-orbit at the end of their life. They are also designed to autonomously avoid collisions based on</div>
<div id="LC704" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">uplinked tracking data.[198]</div>
<div id="LC705" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC706" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In December 2022, SpaceX announced Starshield, a program to incorporate military or government entity</div>
<div id="LC707" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">payloads on board a Starlink-derived satellite bus. The Space Development Agency is a key customer</div>
<div id="LC708" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">procuring satellites for a space-based missile defense system.[199][200]</div>
<div id="LC709" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC710" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2024, SpaceX introduced a compact version of its Starlink antennas, the &quot;Starlink Mini&quot;,</div>
<div id="LC711" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">designed for mobile satellite internet use. Offered for $599 in an early access release, it was more</div>
<div id="LC712" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expensive than the base model. The Mini antenna, half the size and one-third the weight of the Standard</div>
<div id="LC713" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">version, featured a built-in WiFi router, lower power consumption, and over 100 Mbit/s download</div>
<div id="LC714" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">speeds.[201]</div>
<div id="LC715" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC716" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Other projects</div>
<div id="LC717" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC718" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hyperloop</div>
<div id="LC719" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2015, SpaceX announced that it would sponsor a Hyperloop competition, and would build a</div>
<div id="LC720" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1.6 km (0.99 mi) long subscale test track near SpaceX&#39;s headquarters for the competitive events.[202][203]</div>
<div id="LC721" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC722" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The company held the annual competition from 2017 to 2019.[204]</div>
<div id="LC723" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC724" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">COVID-19 antibody-testing program</div>
<div id="LC725" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In collaboration with doctors and academic researchers, SpaceX invited all employees to participate in</div>
<div id="LC726" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the creation of a COVID-19 antibody-testing program in 2020. As such, 4300 employees volunteered to</div>
<div id="LC727" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">provide blood samples resulting in a peer-reviewed scientific paper crediting eight SpaceX employees as</div>
<div id="LC728" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">coauthors and suggesting that a certain level of COVID-19 antibodies may provide lasting protection</div>
<div id="LC729" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">against the virus.[205][206]</div>
<div id="LC730" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC731" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC732" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC733" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Other</div>
<div id="LC734" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2018, Musk arranged for his employees to build a mini-submarine to assist the rescue of children</div>
<div id="LC735" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">stuck in a flooded cavern in Thailand.[207] Richard Stanton, leader of the international rescue diving team,</div>
<div id="LC736" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">encouraged Musk to facilitate the construction of the vehicle as a backup in case flooding worsened.</div>
<div id="LC737" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">However, Stanton later concluded that the mini-submarine would not work and said that Musk&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC738" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">involvement &quot;distracted from the rescue effort&quot;.[208][209] Engineers at SpaceX and The Boring Company</div>
<div id="LC739" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">built the mini-submarine from a Falcon 9 liquid oxygen transfer tube in eight hours and personally</div>
<div id="LC740" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">delivered it to Thailand.[210][211] Thai authorities ultimately declined to use the submarine, stating that it</div>
<div id="LC741" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">wasn&#39;t practical for the rescue mission.[207]</div>
<div id="LC742" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC743" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Facilities</div>
<div id="LC744" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX is headquartered at the SpaceX Starbase near Brownsville, Texas, where it manufactures and</div>
<div id="LC745" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launches its Starship vehicle. However most of the company&#39;s operations are based out of its office in</div>
<div id="LC746" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hawthorne, California where it was previously headquartered, where it builds Falcon rockets and Dragon</div>
<div id="LC747" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft, and where it houses its mission control.</div>
<div id="LC748" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC749" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The company also operates a Starlink satellite manufacturing facilities in Redmond, Washington, a rocket</div>
<div id="LC750" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">development and test facility in McGregor, Texas,[212] and maintains an office in the Washington, D.C.</div>
<div id="LC751" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">area, close to key government customers.[213]</div>
<div id="LC752" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC753" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX has two active launch sites in Florida, one active launch site in California and one active launch</div>
<div id="LC754" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">site at Starbase in Texas.</div>
<div id="LC755" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC756" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hawthorne, CA: Falcon and Dragon manufacturing, mission control</div>
<div id="LC757" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX operates a large facility in the Los Angeles</div>
<div id="LC758" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">suburb of Hawthorne, California. The three-story</div>
<div id="LC759" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">building, originally built by Northrop Corporation to</div>
<div id="LC760" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">build Boeing 747 fuselages,[214] houses SpaceX&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC761" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">office space, mission control, and Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC762" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">manufacturing facilities.[215]</div>
<div id="LC763" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC764" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The area has one of the largest concentrations of</div>
<div id="LC765" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">space sector headquarters, facilities, and subsidiaries</div>
<div id="LC766" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in the U.S., including Boeing/McDonnell Douglas</div>
<div id="LC767" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">main satellite building campuses, The Aerospace SpaceX office in Hawthorne, California at night</div>
<div id="LC768" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Corporation, Raytheon, NASA&#39;s Jet Propulsion during a Falcon 9 launch from Vandenberg Space</div>
<div id="LC769" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC770" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Laboratory, United States Space Force&#39;s Space Force Base</div>
<div id="LC771" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC772" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Systems Command at Los Angeles Air Force Base,</div>
<div id="LC773" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Lockheed Martin, BAE Systems, Northrop Grumman, and AECOM, etc., with a large pool of aerospace</div>
<div id="LC774" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engineers and recent college engineering graduates.[214]</div>
<div id="LC775" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC776" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX uses a high degree of vertical integration in the production of its rockets and rocket engines.[19]</div>
<div id="LC777" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC778" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX builds its rocket engines, rocket stages, spacecraft, principal avionics and all software in-house in</div>
<div id="LC779" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">their Hawthorne facility, which is unusual for the space industry.[19]</div>
<div id="LC780" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC781" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC782" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC783" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The Hawthorne facility was SpaceX&#39;s headquarters until August 2024. However, the move to relocate</div>
<div id="LC784" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s headquarters was seen as largely symbolic, at least in the short term, as the facility will remain</div>
<div id="LC785" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to the company&#39;s operations.[216]</div>
<div id="LC786" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC787" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starbase, TX: Starship manufacturing, launch</div>
<div id="LC788" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX manufactures and flies Starship test vehicles from the</div>
<div id="LC789" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX Starbase in Boca Chica near Brownsville, Texas,</div>
<div id="LC790" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">having announced first plans for the launch facility in August</div>
<div id="LC791" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2014.[217][218] The Federal Aviation Administration (FAA)</div>
<div id="LC792" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">issued the permit in July 2014.[219] SpaceX broke ground on</div>
<div id="LC793" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the new launch facility in 2014 with construction ramping up</div>
<div id="LC794" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in the latter half of 2015,[220] with the first suborbital</div>
<div id="LC795" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launches from the facility in 2019[215] and orbital launches</div>
<div id="LC796" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">starting in 2023.</div>
<div id="LC797" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC798" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The Starship assembly building at</div>
<div id="LC799" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX has faced increased scrutiny over the environmental SpaceX Starbase in Texas</div>
<div id="LC800" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">impact of its Starbase facility.[221][222][223] In August 2024,</div>
<div id="LC801" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Texas Commission on Environmental Quality cited</div>
<div id="LC802" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX for violating environmental regulations by repeatedly releasing pollutants into water near the</div>
<div id="LC803" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Boca Chica launch site.[224] The EPA fined SpaceX approximately $150,000 for allegedly discharging</div>
<div id="LC804" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;industrial wastewater&quot; and violating the Clean Water Act.[225]</div>
<div id="LC805" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC806" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">McGregor, TX: Rocket Development and Test Facility</div>
<div id="LC807" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s Rocket Development and Test Facility in</div>
<div id="LC808" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">McGregor, Texas is a rocket engine test facility. Every</div>
<div id="LC809" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rocket engine and thruster manufactured by SpaceX</div>
<div id="LC810" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">must pass through McGregor for final testing being</div>
<div id="LC811" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">used on flight missions.[226][227] The facility also</div>
<div id="LC812" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">serves as a testing ground for various components and</div>
<div id="LC813" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engines during the research and development</div>
<div id="LC814" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">process.[228] In addition to engine testing, after</div>
<div id="LC815" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">splashdown and recovery, Dragon spacecraft make a</div>
<div id="LC816" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">stop at McGregor to have their hazardous hypergolic</div>
<div id="LC817" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">propellant fuels removed, before the capsules Aerial view of the SpaceX McGregor engine</div>
<div id="LC818" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC819" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">continue on to Hawthorne for refurbishment.[226] testing facility, 2008</div>
<div id="LC820" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC821" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX calls the facility the most advanced and active</div>
<div id="LC822" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rocket engine test facility in the world, and said that as of 2024, over 7,000 tests had been conducted at</div>
<div id="LC823" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the facility since it opened, with seven engine test fires on a typical day, across more than a dozen test</div>
<div id="LC824" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">stands.[229] Despite its low-profile compared to the company&#39;s other facilities, is a critical part of</div>
<div id="LC825" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s operations, and company president and COO Gwynne Shotwell maintains her primary office in</div>
<div id="LC826" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">McGregor.[230][226]</div>
<div id="LC827" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC828" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC829" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC830" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Originally the site of the Bluebonnet Ordnance Plant during World War II,[228] the facility was later used</div>
<div id="LC831" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">by Beal Aerospace before being leased by SpaceX in 2003.[231] The company has since expanded it</div>
<div id="LC832" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">significantly from 256 acres (104 ha) in 2003[228] to 4,000 acres (1,600 ha) by 2015.[229] In July 2021,</div>
<div id="LC833" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX announced plans to build a second production facility for Raptor engines at McGregor. This</div>
<div id="LC834" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expansion is expected to significantly increase SpaceX&#39;s production capacity, with the goal of producing</div>
<div id="LC835" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">800 to 1,000 Raptor engines per year.[232][233]</div>
<div id="LC836" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC837" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink manufacturing facilities</div>
<div id="LC838" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX&#39;s Starlink subsidiary operates over two main facilities. Satellite</div>
<div id="LC839" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">manufacturing takes place near Seattle, Washington while user terminal</div>
<div id="LC840" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">manufacturing takes place near Austin, Texas.</div>
<div id="LC841" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC842" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink&#39;s satellite development and manufacturing operations campus</div>
<div id="LC843" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">occupies over 314,000 square feet (29,200 m2) in at least six buildings</div>
<div id="LC844" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">located in Redmond, Washington, east of Seattle. The first building</div>
<div id="LC845" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">opened in early 2015,[234] and the company later expanded into five</div>
<div id="LC846" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">buildings on the Redmond Ridge Corporate Center.[235][236]</div>
<div id="LC847" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC848" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Starlink opened a user terminal manufacturing facility just outside of</div>
<div id="LC849" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Bastrop, Texas, east of Austin in December 2023. In its first nine</div>
<div id="LC850" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">months of operation, the one-million-square-foot (93,000  m2) facility</div>
<div id="LC851" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">produced one million user terminals and was on track to become the</div>
<div id="LC852" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">largest factory for printed circuit boards in the United States.[237]</div>
<div id="LC853" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC854" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Launch facilities Starlink user terminal on the</div>
<div id="LC855" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sidewalk of a residential home.</div>
<div id="LC856" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC857" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX operates four orbital launch sites, at Cape Canaveral Space</div>
<div id="LC858" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Force Station and Kennedy Space Center in Florida and</div>
<div id="LC859" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Vandenberg Space Force Base in California for Falcon</div>
<div id="LC860" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rockets, and Starbase near Brownsville, Texas for Starship.</div>
<div id="LC861" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX has indicated that they see a niche for each of the</div>
<div id="LC862" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">four orbital facilities and that they have sufficient launch</div>
<div id="LC863" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">business to fill each pad.[238] The Vandenberg launch site</div>
<div id="LC864" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">enables highly inclined orbits (66–145°), while Cape</div>
<div id="LC865" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Canaveral and Kennedy enable orbits of medium inclination</div>
<div id="LC866" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(28.5–55°).[239] Larger inclinations, including SSO, are</div>
<div id="LC867" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">possible from Florida by overflying Cuba.[240] Falcon Heavy Side Boosters landing on</div>
<div id="LC868" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC869" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">LZ1 and LZ2 at Cape Canaveral</div>
<div id="LC870" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC871" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Before it was retired, all Falcon 1 launches took place at the</div>
<div id="LC872" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Ronald Reagan Ballistic Missile Defense Test Site on Omelek</div>
<div id="LC873" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Island of the Marshall Islands.[241]</div>
<div id="LC874" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC875" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2007, the Pentagon approved the use of Cape Canaveral Space Launch Complex 40 (SLC-40) by</div>
<div id="LC876" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX.[242] The site has been used since 2010 for Falcon 9 launches, mainly to low Earth and</div>
<div id="LC877" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">geostationary orbits. The former Launch Complex 13 at Cape Canaveral, now renamed Landing Zones 1</div>
<div id="LC878" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC879" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC880" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC881" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and 2, has since 2015 been used for Falcon 9 first-stage booster landings.[243] Later on, SpaceX will retire</div>
<div id="LC882" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">these two landing zones and add three landing zones for Falcon 9 and Falcon Heavy rockets to conduct to</div>
<div id="LC883" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;Return-to-launch-site&quot; landings, two at LC-39A and one at SLC-40.[244][245][246]</div>
<div id="LC884" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC885" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Vandenberg Space Launch Complex 4 (SLC-4E) was leased</div>
<div id="LC886" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">from the military in 2011 and is used for payloads to polar</div>
<div id="LC887" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">orbits. The Vandenberg site can launch both Falcon 9 and</div>
<div id="LC888" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon Heavy vehicles,[247] but cannot launch to low</div>
<div id="LC889" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">inclination orbits. The neighboring SLC-4W was converted to</div>
<div id="LC890" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Landing Zone 4 in 2015 for booster landings.[248]</div>
<div id="LC891" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC892" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On April 14, 2014, SpaceX signed a 20-year lease for</div>
<div id="LC893" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Kennedy Space Center Launch Complex 39A.[249] The pad</div>
<div id="LC894" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was subsequently modified to support Falcon 9 and Falcon SpaceX west coast launch facility at</div>
<div id="LC895" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC896" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Vandenberg Space Force Base, during</div>
<div id="LC897" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Heavy launches. As of 2024 it is the only pad that supports the launch of CASSIOPE</div>
<div id="LC898" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon Heavy launches. SpaceX launched its first crewed</div>
<div id="LC899" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">mission to the ISS from Launch Pad 39A on May 30,</div>
<div id="LC900" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2020.[250] Pad 39A has been prepared since 2019 to eventually accommodate Starship launches. With</div>
<div id="LC901" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">delays in launch FAA permits for Boca Chica, Texas, the 39A Starship preparation was accelerated in</div>
<div id="LC902" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2022.[251]</div>
<div id="LC903" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC904" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Contracts</div>
<div id="LC905" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX won demonstration and actual supply contracts from NASA for the International Space Station</div>
<div id="LC906" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(ISS) with technology the company developed. SpaceX is also certified for U.S. military launches of</div>
<div id="LC907" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Evolved Expendable Launch Vehicle-class (EELV) payloads. With approximately thirty missions on the</div>
<div id="LC908" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">manifest for 2018 alone, SpaceX represented over $12 billion under contract.[70]</div>
<div id="LC909" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC910" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Cargo to ISS</div>
<div id="LC911" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2006, SpaceX won a NASA Commercial Orbital</div>
<div id="LC912" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Transportation Services (COTS) Phase 1 contract to</div>
<div id="LC913" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">demonstrate cargo delivery to the ISS, with a possible</div>
<div id="LC914" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contract option for crew transport.[252] Through this contract,</div>
<div id="LC915" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">designed by NASA to provide &quot;seed money&quot; through Space</div>
<div id="LC916" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Act Agreements for developing new capabilities, NASA paid</div>
<div id="LC917" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX $396  million to develop the cargo configuration of</div>
<div id="LC918" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Dragon spacecraft, while SpaceX developed the Falcon 9</div>
<div id="LC919" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launch vehicle with their resources.[253] These Space Act</div>
<div id="LC920" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Agreements have been shown to have saved NASA millions</div>
<div id="LC921" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of dollars in development costs, making rocket development The COTS 2 Dragon is berthed to the</div>
<div id="LC922" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">4–10 times less expensive than if produced by NASA International Space Station (ISS) by</div>
<div id="LC923" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">alone.[254] Canadarm2</div>
<div id="LC924" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC925" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC926" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC927" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In December 2010, with the launch of the SpaceX COTS Demo Flight 1 mission, SpaceX became the</div>
<div id="LC928" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">first private company to successfully launch, orbit, and recover a spacecraft.[255] Dragon successfully</div>
<div id="LC929" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">berthed with the ISS during SpaceX COTS Demo Flight 2 in May 2012, a first for a private</div>
<div id="LC930" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft.[256]</div>
<div id="LC931" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC932" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Commercial Resupply Services (CRS) is a series of contracts awarded by NASA from 2008 to 2016 for</div>
<div id="LC933" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the delivery of cargo and supplies to the ISS on commercially operated spacecraft. The first CRS</div>
<div id="LC934" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contracts were signed in 2008 and awarded $1.6  billion to SpaceX for 12 cargo transport missions,</div>
<div id="LC935" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">covering deliveries to 2016.[257] SpaceX CRS-1, the first of the 12 planned resupply missions, launched</div>
<div id="LC936" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in October 2012, achieved orbit, berthed, and remained on station for 20 days, before re-entering the</div>
<div id="LC937" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">atmosphere and splashing down in the Pacific Ocean.[258]</div>
<div id="LC938" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC939" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">CRS missions have flown approximately twice a year to the ISS since then. In 2015, NASA extended the</div>
<div id="LC940" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Phase 1 contracts by ordering an additional three resupply flights from SpaceX, and then extended the</div>
<div id="LC941" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">contract further for a total of twenty cargo missions to the ISS.[259][257][260] The final Dragon 1 mission,</div>
<div id="LC942" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX CRS-20, departed the ISS in April 2020, and Dragon was subsequently retired from service. A</div>
<div id="LC943" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">second phase of contracts was awarded in January 2016 with SpaceX as one of the awardees. SpaceX will</div>
<div id="LC944" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">fly up to nine additional CRS flights with the upgraded Dragon 2 spacecraft.[261][262] In March 2020,</div>
<div id="LC945" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">NASA contracted SpaceX to develop the Dragon XL spacecraft to send supplies to the Lunar Gateway</div>
<div id="LC946" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">space station. Dragon XL will be launched on a Falcon Heavy.[263]</div>
<div id="LC947" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC948" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Crewed</div>
<div id="LC949" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX is responsible for the transportation of NASA</div>
<div id="LC950" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">astronauts to and from the ISS. The NASA contracts</div>
<div id="LC951" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">started as part of the Commercial Crew Development</div>
<div id="LC952" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(CCDev) program, aimed at developing commercially</div>
<div id="LC953" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operated spacecraft capable of delivering astronauts to</div>
<div id="LC954" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the ISS. The first contract was awarded to SpaceX in</div>
<div id="LC955" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2011,[264][265] followed by another in 2012 to</div>
<div id="LC956" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">continue development and testing of its Dragon 2</div>
<div id="LC957" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">spacecraft.[266]</div>
<div id="LC958" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC959" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In September 2014, NASA chose SpaceX and Boeing NASA astronauts inside the Dragon spacecraft</div>
<div id="LC960" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">as the two companies that would be funded to develop during the Crew-1 mission rendezvous with the</div>
<div id="LC961" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">systems to transport U.S. crews to and from the International Space Station</div>
<div id="LC962" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC963" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ISS.[267] SpaceX won $2.6  billion to complete and</div>
<div id="LC964" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">certify Dragon 2 by 2017. The contracts called for at least one crewed flight test with at least one NASA</div>
<div id="LC965" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">astronaut aboard. Once Crew Dragon received NASA human-spaceflight certification, the contract</div>
<div id="LC966" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">required SpaceX to conduct at least two, and as many as six, crewed missions to the space station.[267]</div>
<div id="LC967" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC968" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX completed the first key flight test of its Crew Dragon spacecraft, a Pad Abort Test, in May</div>
<div id="LC969" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2015,[268] and successfully conducted a full uncrewed test flight in early 2019. The capsule docked to the</div>
<div id="LC970" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ISS and then splashed down in the Atlantic Ocean.[269] In January 2020, SpaceX conducted an in-flight</div>
<div id="LC971" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">abort test, the last test flight before flying crew, in which the Dragon spacecraft fired its launch escape</div>
<div id="LC972" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engines in a simulated abort scenario.[270]</div>
<div id="LC973" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC974" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC975" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC976" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On May 30, 2020, the Crew Dragon Demo-2 mission was launched to the International Space Station</div>
<div id="LC977" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with NASA astronauts Bob Behnken and Doug Hurley, the first time a crewed vehicle had launched from</div>
<div id="LC978" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the U.S. since 2011, and the first SpaceX commercial crewed launch to the ISS.[271] The Crew-1 mission</div>
<div id="LC979" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was successfully launched to the International Space Station on November 16, 2020, with NASA</div>
<div id="LC980" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">astronauts Michael Hopkins, Victor Glover and Shannon Walker along with JAXA astronaut Soichi</div>
<div id="LC981" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Noguchi,[272] all members of the Expedition 64 crew.[273] On April 23, 2021, Crew-2 was launched to the</div>
<div id="LC982" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">International Space Station with NASA astronauts Shane Kimbrough and K. Megan McArthur, JAXA</div>
<div id="LC983" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">astronaut Akihiko Hoshide, and ESA astronaut Thomas Pesquet.[274] The Crew-2 mission successfully</div>
<div id="LC984" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">docked on April 24, 2021.[275]</div>
<div id="LC985" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC986" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">SpaceX also offers paid crewed spaceflights for</div>
<div id="LC987" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">private individuals. The first of these missions,</div>
<div id="LC988" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Inspiration4, launched in 2021 on behalf of Shift4</div>
<div id="LC989" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Payments CEO Jared Isaacman. The mission launched</div>
<div id="LC990" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Crew Dragon Resilience from the Florida</div>
<div id="LC991" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Kennedy Space Center&#39;s Launch Complex 39A atop a</div>
<div id="LC992" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Falcon 9 launch vehicle, placed the Dragon capsule</div>
<div id="LC993" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">into low Earth orbit, and ended successfully about</div>
<div id="LC994" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">three days later when the Resilience splashed down in</div>
<div id="LC995" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Atlantic Ocean. All four crew members received Resilience after splashdown</div>
<div id="LC996" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">commercial astronaut training from SpaceX. The</div>
<div id="LC997" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">training included lessons in orbital mechanics,</div>
<div id="LC998" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operating in a microgravity environment, stress testing, emergency-preparedness training, and mission</div>
<div id="LC999" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">simulations.[276]</div>
<div id="LC1000" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
